# Compiler Bugbash Progress

- 2026-07-12: Side-effecting conditional assignments and nested conditional branch conditions now lower into explicit semantic `Branch` operations with typed temporaries, preserving CUDA lazy `?:` evaluation instead of emitting eager WGSL `select`. Storage-pointer helper atomics now use the same typed pointer ABI in semantic reference and WGSL; conditional calls not yet materialized in initializers, returns, arguments, or loop conditions remain fail-closed on the AST backend. Disabled/enabled semantic reference proof preserved storage/output `[2,0]` and `[9,9]`; compiler passed `832/832`; browser passed `181/181`; `verify:compiler` passed. CUDA-samples remains semantic-WebGPU `341/357` with true AST fallback `13`; baseline `03008c77` reproduced the same four auto-corpus native failures, proving no new native regression in this slice.
- 2026-07-12: `bg_subgroup_add` now participates in the shared semantic subgroup registry, arity contract, arithmetic-reduction classification, reference evaluator, and WGSL operand selection. Scalar compatibility mode bypasses cooperative scratch reducers and lowers every arithmetic subgroup alias to identity semantics through semantic IR; exact native WebGPU output passed `[1,2,3,4]`. Native mode deliberately remains on the validated backend: takeover testing exposed unsafe workgroup-barrier helpers inside divergent loops, so semantic native support stays blocked until explicit uniformization exists. Cooperative tests passed `112/112`; browser suite passed `181/181`; internal compiler fallback invocations moved `219 -> 217`; expression blockers moved `7 -> 5`.
- 2026-07-12: Mutable storage-pointer statement forms `p += n`, `p -= n`, `p++`, and `p--` now lower into the same typed pointer-offset IR used by assignment rebases. Semantic reference and WGSL mutate explicit per-invocation offsets; no pointer value is stored or simulated. Full compiler unit passed `832/832`; browser suite passed `181/181`. Internal compiler fallback invocations moved `220 -> 219`; expression blockers moved `8 -> 7`.
- 2026-07-12: Kernel-side `cudaGetDeviceFlags` and `cudaSetDeviceFlags` now lower as explicit WebGPU policy semantics: getter writes zero flags and returns success; setter returns success without inventing mutable native device state. Typed semantic assignments/stores feed reference and WGSL. Full compiler unit passed `832/832`; browser suite passed `181/181`. Internal compiler fallback invocations moved `221 -> 220`; expression blockers moved `9 -> 8`.
- 2026-07-12: CUDA `sincosf`, `__sincosf`, and `sincospi` output parameters now share the semantic math out-target contract for local scalars and modeled memory. IR freezes each angle once, then emits explicit sine/cosine assignments; reference and WGSL no longer depend on AST out-param lowering. Full compiler unit passed `832/832`; browser suite passed `181/181`. Internal compiler fallback invocations moved `222 -> 221`; call blockers moved `43 -> 42`.
- 2026-07-12: PTX `isspacep.{global,shared,const,local}` now resolves from semantic address spaces into typed IR constants. Reference and WGSL consume the resulting ordinary assignments; no runtime pointer tagging or AST inline-asm node remains. Full compiler unit passed `832/832`; browser suite passed `181/181`. Internal compiler fallback invocations moved `223 -> 222`; raw inline-asm blockers moved `1 -> 0`.

## How To Track This

Use this file as the source of truth for the current bugbash.

- Quick status, latest proof, active failures, and remaining probes: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run bugbash:status`
- Replay current blocker: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run e2e:webgpu:last-failures`
- See scoped test plan: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run verify:changed:plan`
- Read `Dashboard` first for the state in one screen.
- Read `Latest Proven Green Gates` before trusting any "fixed" claim.
- Read `Bugs Found During Current Run` to see root fixes, not shallow patches.
- Read `Remaining Probe Map` to see what is still not proven.
- Read `Gate Ladder` to see why I am running a focused command instead of a repo-wide command.

Every work chunk should update:

- `Last updated`
- `Dashboard.Active work item`
- `Latest Proven Green Gates`
- `Bugs Found During Current Run`
- `Remaining Probe Map`

Done means all of these are true:

- No current failing focused cases.
- No new skips in touched compiler tests or WebGPU fixtures.
- Full compiler unit suite passes, not only filtered `-t` runs.
- WebGPU smoke passes with `0 skipped`.
- Source and dist real-world verifier pass with `0 skipped`.
- Remaining unsupported cases have explicit diagnostics, not emit-time crashes or silent wrong output.

Fixed-rank shared arrays emit one flat row-major `var<workgroup>` array, reusing semantic IR's existing flattened index contract; multidimensional shared arrays passed through pointer helpers remain deliberately rejected until that pointer ABI is flattened. CUDA-samples semantic-direct lowering is now `188/354`, with `357/357` plan compilation and `0` hard failures. Full `verify:compiler` passed, compiler unit tests passed `761/0`, and real WebGPU tests passed `110/0`.
- Cooperative barriers and typed cache-hint loads: `cg::sync(block)` is included in divergent-return barrier safety analysis and modeled as a uniform semantic barrier; cooperative-group declarations are semantic-reference metadata. Cache-hint pointer lowering now preserves vector reinterpret-view type and lane scaling through address-of/index aliases, so `__ldcs(&float4View[idx])` emits a native `vec4<f32>` load rather than a scalar mismatch. WGSL identifiers reserve `pass`, and the changed-test router now executes the compiler test directory rather than a stale removed filename. Focused unit coverage passed `3/0`; exact native WebGPU cache-hint fixture passed `1/0/0`; full compiler verification passed `761/0`; browser suite passed `110/0`.
- Semantic dynamic-launch child signature slice: semantic models now publish typed, source-spanned `launchableEntries` for kernel and device-function child targets; host-dynamic planning resolves child names and binds child input from this semantic inventory, not `compiled.ast.kernels` or `compiled.ast.functions`; focused dynamic-launch suite passed `26/0`, typecheck passed, and lint passed. Parent launch traversal and host expression evaluation remain AST-shaped work, explicitly tracked below.
- Semantic grid-sync backend repair: dynamic shared-memory extents now live in `kernelIr.memory`, semantic WGSL validates memory indices and loop initializers with semantic module context, scalar compound assignments include shifts and bitwise forms, and block/grid cooperative `thread_rank()`/`size()` queries emit natively. Focused real WebGPU CUDA-120 grid-sync fixtures passed `6/0/0`; semantic `cg::reduce` helper lowering remains unfinished for one cuda-samples fixture.

## Remaining Probe Map

- Semantic function-param scope still uses source spellings in several backend lookup helpers. Direct storage-byte collisions now receive owner-qualified link names and expressions preserve resolved symbol names, but full function ownership must move into semantic symbol identity before legacy adapters are removed; broad renaming currently regresses shared-pointer specialization and is not an acceptable shortcut.
- Kernel scalar/vector params now preserve CUDA pass-by-value mutation through explicit semantic local shadows. Remaining parameter work is optional/null pointer presence and aggregate/object params; do not model pointer truthiness as always true merely because WebGPU bindings exist.
- Local-pointer aliasing now covers typed dereference offsets, nested-loop storage rebinding, direct storage-byte helper params, and fixed-slot pointer arrays with explicit scalar-lane/vector-element base units. Dynamic pointer-array indices, conditional/rebound roots, multi-dimensional views, and runtime pointer-array routing remain unsupported until semantic IR carries explicit handle selection; every new form needs semantic-reference parity, a semantic WGSL structural assertion, and real WebGPU proof.
- Semantic reference: direct uniform-loop barriers, including `cg::sync(block)`, now have resumable IR scheduling. Extend this only to device-helper barriers after function-call continuations and lexical local restoration have matching scheduler semantics; do not bypass the current explicit rejection. Do not classify generic member `sync` as a block barrier: grid-sync remains a host-phased operation.
- Architecture: shared contracts cover identical adapter rules only. Next audit candidate is typed memory-reference eligibility; do not merge it until address-space/vector-lane differences can be expressed without weakening either adapter. Surface-object pointer arrays require an explicit indexed-surface handle contract rather than scalar surface lowering.
- Runtime planning: grid-sync consumes semantic IR end to end for simple/block-grid reductions, but semantic `cg::reduce` helper lowering is still required before the cuda-samples grid-sync fixture can rejoin the full native browser gate. Dynamic launch now resolves child signatures from semantic IR, but parent launch traversal and host expression evaluation remain AST-shaped; migrate those next before replacing `CudaHostDynamicLaunch.statement` with `SemanticDeviceLaunch`. Peer-copy planning still contains AST-shape helpers and needs equivalent semantic operation checks.

Probe these with fail-first real WebGPU fixtures:

- Safety family:
  - scalar-guarded CUDA `__trap` launch preconditions are now modeled as explicit host-evaluable contracts over scalar kernel params and CUDA named constants; failed or unknown preconditions reject before WebGPU dispatch and before CPU reference execution, valid preconditions now have native browser WebGPU `single-dispatch` fixture coverage, and lane/device-dependent traps remain `unsupported-device-trap` until BrowserGrad has a real device abort contract
- Runtime family:
  - 1D/2D host/device/default copies, peer copies, symbol copies including byte-granular counts/offsets, symbol byte-pattern fills, byte-pattern `cudaMemset`/`cudaMemsetAsync`/`cudaMemset2D`/`cudaMemset2DAsync` fills, stream/event create deterministic handle writes, stream capture-info v1/v2 queries, occupancy available dynamic shared-memory queries, stream capture graph lifecycle calls (`cudaStreamBeginCapture`, `cudaStreamEndCapture`, `cudaStreamUpdateCaptureDependencies`, `cudaGraphDestroy`), and graph create/instantiate/update lifecycle calls (`cudaGraphCreate`, `cudaGraphInstantiate`, `cudaGraphInstantiateWithFlags`, `cudaGraphUpload`, `cudaGraphExecUpdate`, `cudaGraphExecDestroy`) are now modeled through reference plus native WebGPU-safe host/runtime planning; keep widening remaining runtime calls only when they have explicit host orchestration semantics or clear diagnostics
- CUDA library/helper family:
  - `cufftComplex`/`float2` pointwise helpers, `cuComplex`/`cuFloatComplex` helper builtins, and `cuDoubleComplex` f32-compat helper aliases are now native reference/WGSL/WebGPU capabilities with pinned complex fixtures; `cuCabsf`/`cuCdivf` and their double-helper aliases use scaled formulas for large finite values instead of overflow-prone WGSL `length`/naive denominator math; `cuCfmaf`/`cuCfma` now have native complex fused multiply-add lowering; keep widening library-adjacent helpers as vertical slices with analyzer contracts, reference semantics, WGSL lowering, and exact real WebGPU output fixtures
  - Scalar half directed conversion aliases, short/ushort conversion and bitcast helpers, scalar and half2 unary math aliases, half2 lane extraction/packing helpers, scalar/half2 ordered/unordered comparison vectors, scalar/half2 NaN/Inf predicates, bool reductions, mask aliases, NaN-propagating min/max aliases, scalar/half2 `_rn` and saturating arithmetic aliases, scalar bf16 unary/arithmetic/predicate/minmax aliases, scalar bf16 narrow integer conversion aliases, bf16 ll/ull conversion aliases under the current 32-bit integer ABI, explicit f32-compatible `__double2bfloat16`, bf162 lane/vector conversion aliases, bf162 unary math aliases, bf162 arithmetic aliases, and bf162 comparison/minmax aliases now lower through analyzer/reference/semantic reference/semantic WGSL to native WebGPU shader-f16, f32-compat, or bf16-as-f32 storage paths; directed conversion aliases preserve rn/rz/ru/rd half/bf16 bit rounding, and short/ushort/char/uchar helpers preserve explicit rounding plus 8-bit/16-bit wrap/sign-extension semantics; saturating aliases preserve CUDA clamp-to-`[0,1]` plus NaN-to-`+0` semantics; comparison aliases preserve ordered/unordered NaN behavior and mask lane bits; NaN-propagating min/max aliases preserve CUDA NaN pass-through semantics; lane helpers preserve CUDA low/high lane extraction and packing semantics; bf162 unary math preserves lane-wise bf16 rounding and round-to-even `h2rint`; keep widening half/bf16-family helpers only with explicit rounding/type semantics and browser WebGPU proof, keep true f64 unsupported unless explicit f32 compatibility is requested, and keep true i64 unsupported until a real browser-native or explicit polyfill contract exists
  - `__dp4a` plus `__dp2a_lo`/`__dp2a_hi` signed and unsigned packed integer dot-add overloads plus packed halfword/byte SIMD add/sub/saturating-add/saturating-sub/absdiff/avg/min/max/set/compare/abs/neg/SAD/viadd/vimax/vimin/vib intrinsics now lower through semantic IR/reference/WGSL to native WebGPU scalar integer ops with exact real WebGPU fixtures `intrinsic:dp4a`, `intrinsic:dp2a`, `intrinsic:simd-byte`, `intrinsic:simd-halfword`, `intrinsic:simd-unsigned-saturated`, `intrinsic:simd-signed-saturated`, `intrinsic:simd-minmax`, `intrinsic:simd-vset`, `intrinsic:simd-vcmp`, `intrinsic:simd-abs-sad`, `intrinsic:simd-avg`, `intrinsic:viadd-minmax`, `intrinsic:viminmax-relu3`, and `intrinsic:vib-predicate`; keep widening integer/library intrinsics with signedness-aware semantics, not blind name-only lowering
- Subgroup family:

## Gate Ladder

Use this order:

1. Target case: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run e2e:webgpu:case -- --cases <case>`
2. Focused unit: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler test -- --runInBand -t <name>`
3. Type/lint: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run typecheck && pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run lint`
4. Smoke: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run e2e:webgpu:smoke`
5. Full source: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run e2e:webgpu -- --require-webgpu --forbid-skips --summary-only --case-timeout-ms 30000`
6. Verifier: `pnpm --filter @unlocalhosted/browsergrad-compiler run verify:real-world-cuda -- --skip-fetch --require-webgpu`

## Reporting Format

End each work chunk with:

- Found: bug/no bug
- Changed: files + root behavior
- Proof: exact counts
- Remaining: next 1-3 probes
- Git: staged/unstaged/dirty
- 2026-07-11: Cooperative tile `shfl`/`shfl_down`/`shfl_up`/`shfl_xor` member calls now lower into typed semantic shuffle intrinsics carrying explicit logical tile width. Semantic reference executes collective-bearing branches/loops in lockstep so peer locals reach the same program point; semantic WGSL hoists workgroup-memory shuffle calls out of divergent first-tile guards and predicates only state mutation, keeping every invocation on uniform barriers. CUDA value-producing chained assignments now lower into explicit semantic sequences instead of invalid WGSL assignment expressions, and multidimensional tile rank/metadata arithmetic now parenthesizes the full linear rank before modulo/division. Exact synthetic first-tile native WebGPU passed `1/1`; exact cuda-samples `reduce4<256>` passed native WebGPU with output `[512]`; changed-fast passed compiler unit `770/770` and fast auto-corpus `32/32`; full compiler passed `786/786`; source/dist real-world WebGPU passed `857/857` each with fixtures `128/128`, zero failures, and zero skips. CUDA-samples semantic-direct moved `282/354 -> 287/354`, AST fallback `72 -> 67`; `reduce4`, `reduce5`, `reduce6`, `reduceFinal`, and one additional logical-tile kernel are semantic-direct. `finalError` advanced past shuffle lowering to its independent atomic blocker.

- 2026-07-12: Inline PTX 32-bit add/sub/mul.lo/mad.lo, shifts, signed/unsigned min/max, neg, and abs now lower to explicit semantic PTX calls with one shared operation contract. Semantic reference reuses exact `Math.imul`/bitwise evaluators; semantic WGSL reuses overflow-safe and overshift-safe emitters. Native WebGPU edge proof passed `[0x70b88d78,0xffffffff,0xffffffff,0x80000000]` for mul overflow, signed overshift, signed min, and `abs(INT_MIN)`. Compiler unit passed `832/832`; browser suite passed `181/181`. Internal fallback tracing moved `244 -> 236` compile invocations; store blockers moved `52 -> 44`.
